              Management Discussion and Analysis                                               7

業務回顧                          BUSINESS REVIEW
概覽                            Overview

我們是一家致力於釀造高品質白酒產品的中國領先        We are a leading baijiu company in China devoted to offering
白酒公司。我們生產及銷售醬香型、兼香型及濃香        high-quality baijiu products. We produce and sell sauce aroma,
型白酒，其中醬香型白酒是我們的主要增長引擎。        mixed aroma and strong aroma baijiu, with sauce aroma baijiu
                              serving as our major growth engine.


於 2025 年，白酒行業面臨若干挑戰，消費需求持     The baijiu industry faced certain challenges in 2025, with
續走弱。面對嚴峻的經濟環境和低迷的白酒消費需        consumption demand weakening continuously. In response
求，我們持續降低渠道庫存並維持健康的價格體系        to the challenging economic environment and subdued baijiu
和經銷商盈利能力。我們的努力在 2026 年上半年     consumption demand, we continued to reduce channel inventories
開始顯現成效。以珍十五為代表的珍酒 傳統核心產       while maintaining healthy pricing systems and distributor
品，其價格體系和渠道庫存已於期內恢復至健康水        profitability. Our efforts began to bear fruit in the first half of
平。李渡 在2026年上半年實現收入增長28.9%，湘   2026. The pricing systems and channel inventories for Zhen Jiu’s
窖 及開口笑 均企穩並錄得溫和增長。2026 年對本    traditional core products, represented by Zhen 15, returned to
集團而言是戰略關鍵之年，標誌著為期兩年的行業        healthy levels during the period. Li Du achieved revenue growth
下行期導致的業務收縮的結束，以及新的增長週期        of 28.9% in the first half of 2026, while Xiang Jiao and Kai Kou
的開始，是本集團業務整體企穩回升的轉折點。於        Xiao each stabilized and recorded modest growth. The year of
2026年，我們不僅要從週期低谷中反彈，更要為進      2026 is strategically critical for the Group, marking the end of the
入未來的增長週期奠定堅實的業務基礎。            contraction of our business resulting from the two-year industry
                              downturn and the beginning of a new cycle of renewed growth. It
                              is also an inflection point at which the Group’s overall operations
                              are stabilizing and recovering. In 2026, we intend not only to
                              rebound from the trough of the cycle, but also to lay a solid
                              business foundation for entering a future growth cycle.

我們的品牌及產品                                   Our Brands and Products

我們已建立三層增長引擎。我們的旗艦品牌珍酒 ，                    We have developed a three-tier growth engine. Zhen Jiu , our
以次高端及以上價格範圍的醬香型白酒為特色，是                     flagship brand featuring premium and above sauce aroma baijiu,
我們的主要增長引擎，抓住了醬香型白酒市場的強                     serves as our major growth engine, capturing the strong growth
勁增長潛力。李渡 是我們的第二增長引擎，亦是一                    potential of the sauce aroma baijiu market. Li Du , our second
個以次高端及以上價格範圍為主的兼香型白酒產品                     growth engine and a brand mainly featuring premium and above
為特色的品牌，且已實現高速增長；隨著我們堅定                     mixed aroma baijiu products, has delivered high growth and is
不移地進一步提升其品牌知名度和拓展江西省以外                     expected to create additional momentum for continued growth as
地區的全國銷售網絡，預期其將為持續增長創造額                     we remain determined to further strengthen its brand recognition
外動力。湘窖 和開口笑 是我們在湖南市場的區域領                   and nationwide sales network outside Jiangxi Province. Xiang
先品牌，預期將繼續為我們的長期可持續增長作出                     Jiao and Kai Kou Xiao , our regional leading brands in the Hunan
穩定且持續的貢獻。我們對該四個白酒品牌進行戰                     market, are expected to make steady and ongoing contributions
略性定位，以滿足中國不同的消費者喜好及地理區                     to our long-term sustainable growth. We tactically position these

                           區域擴張與滲透                              Regional Expansion and Penetration

                           隨著我們為應對嚴峻行業環境而持續優化渠道結                As our sustained efforts to optimise channel structures and
                           構、調整區域市場戰略的努力漸見成效，我們以明               recalibrate regional market strategies in response to the
                           確的戰略轉向進入2026年，即在縱向及橫向兩個維             challenging industry environment have progressively taken effect,
                           度推進區域擴張及更深入的市場滲透。縱向下沉指               we entered 2026 with a clear strategic pivot towards regional
                           將我們的觸達範圍從成熟的核心市場下沉至該等區               expansion and deeper market penetration on both a vertical and
                           域內尚未開發的低線城市及鄉鎮。我們優先選擇品               a horizontal basis. Vertical penetration refers to penetrating our
                           牌已在相關省份的省會及其他重點城市建立較高知               reach from established core markets into untapped lower-tier
                           名度的市場，使我們能夠利用已有知名度，更高效               cities and townships within those regions. We prioritise markets

                           認為具有較大市場發展潛力的非核心省份及地區。               in the provincial capital and other key cities within the relevant
                                                                province, enabling us to leverage that established presence

                                                                expansion refers to selectively entering non-core provinces and
                                                                regions where we see significant market development potential.

非國際財務報告準則計量                   Non-IFRS Measures

為補充我們根據國際財務報告會計準則呈列的綜合        To supplement our consolidated financial statements that are
財務報表，我們亦採用經調整淨利潤（非國際財務        presented in accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards, we
報告準則計量）及經調整淨利率（非國際財務報告準       also use adjusted net profit (non-IFRS measure) and adjusted
則計量）作為額外財務計量，有關計量並非國際財        net profit margin (non-IFRS measure) as additional financial
務報告會計準則所規定或按國際財務報告會計準則        measures, which are not required by, or presented in accordance
呈列。我們相信，該等非國際財務報告準則計量有        with, IFRS Accounting Standards. We believe that these non-IFRS
助於通過剔除若干項目的潛在影響，比較不同期間        measures facilitate comparisons of operating performance from
及不同公司的經營表現。我們相信，該等計量為投        period to period and company to company by eliminating potential
資者及其他人士提供有用信息，以與幫助管理層相        impacts of certain items. We believe that these measures provide
同的方式了解及評估我們的綜合經營業績。然而，        useful information to investors and others in understanding and
我們呈列的經調整淨利潤（非國際財務報告準則計        evaluating our consolidated results of operations in the same
量）及經調整淨利率（非國際財務報告準則計量）未       manner as they help our management. However, our presentation
必可與其他公司所呈列的類似名稱的計量比較。該        of adjusted net profit (non-IFRS measure) and adjusted net profit
等非國際財務報告準則計量用作分析工具存在局限        margin (non-IFRS measure) may not be comparable to similarly
性， 閣下不應視其為獨立於我們根據國際財務報告       titled measures presented by other companies. The use of these
會計準則呈報的經營業績或財務狀況或可代替相關        non-IFRS measures has limitations as an analytical tool, and
分析。                           you should not consider it in isolation from, or as substitute for
                              analysis of, our results of operations or financial condition as
                              reported under IFRS Accounting Standards.

環境、社會和治理(ESG)                 ENVIRONMENT, SOCIAL AND GOVERNANCE (ESG)
ESG工作概覽                       ESG Work Overview

面向高質量發展的新階段，集團持續深化ESG治理       As the Group enters a new stage of high-quality development,
體系建設，將ESG要求深度融入戰略決策與日常運       we continue to deepen our ESG governance framework and
營，制定了更高標準、更具牽引性的年度ESG目標       further integrate ESG requirements into strategic decision-
與行動方案，並將相關指標逐級細分至各運營單位        making and day-to-day operations. We have formulated annual
和部門，納入集團績效體系，與員工薪酬激勵相掛        ESG targets and action plans with higher standards and stronger
鈎，系統地持續追蹤並考核ESG重點工作的關鍵進       strategic focus, cascaded the relevant indicators to each
展和成效。通過持續監控ESG指標並推進一系列        operational unit and department, and incorporated them into

                           健康與安全：2026 年，我們進一步加強了員工職             Health and Safety: In 2026, we further strengthened occupational

                           圍，做到安全責任無盲區、管理無死角。各酒廠結               personnel entering our distilleries, continuing to broaden the
                           合生產運營特點常態化開展職業健康安全培訓，並               coverage of safety management, ensuring full coverage of
                           於2026年6月圍繞「安全月」組織隱患排查、應急演            safety responsibilities without blind spot, and eliminating gaps
                           練、安全知識競賽及專項培訓等系列活動，強化風               in management. In line with their respective production and
                           險識別與應急處置能力；同時通過常態化「安全周」              operating characteristics, all distilleries conducted regular OHS

                           常運營環節。隨著「回廠遊」持續發展以及廠區擴               activities, including hazard inspections, emergency drills, safety-
                           建、改建項目推進，集團進一步加強外來人員和施               knowledge contests and specialized training, to strengthen

                           知、現場防護到過程管理的安全管控，切實保障每               embed safety requirements into day-to-day operations. With the

                           線。                                   progress of plant expansion and renovation projects, the Group

首次公開發售後股權激勵計劃                   POST-IPO EQUITY INCENTIVE PLAN
首次公開發售後股權激勵計劃於2023 年4 月11 日通    The Post-IPO Equity Incentive Plan was approved by way of
過股東決議案獲批准，自上市日期起生效。             shareholders’ resolution on April 11, 2023, with effect from the
                                Listing Date.


首次公開發售後股權激勵計劃的目的是激勵合資格          The purpose of the Post-IPO Equity Incentive Plan is to incentivize
參與者（定義見下文）並就彼等對本集團作出的貢獻         and reward the Eligible Participants (as defined below) for their
給予獎勵，使彼等的利益與本公司利益一致，藉此          contribution to the Group and to align their interests with that of
鼓勵彼等努力提升本公司價值。                  our Company so as to encourage them to work towards enhancing
                                the value of our Company.


董事會（就本段而言，包括董事會或其正式授權的委         The Board (which expression shall, for the purpose of this
員會）可全權酌情向(a)本公司或其任何附屬公司的        paragraph, include the Board or a duly authorized committee
僱員（不論全職或兼職）或董事及 (b) 本公司控股公      thereof) may, at its absolute discretion, offer to grant an option
司、同系附屬公司或聯營公司的董事及僱員（第(a)        or a share award to subscribe for such number of Shares as the
及(b)項，統稱為「合資格參與者」）授出購股權或股       Board may determine to (a) an employee (whether full time or
份獎勵以認購董事會所釐定數目的股份。              part-time) or a director of our Company or any of its subsidiaries
                                and (b) directors and employees of the holding companies, fellow
                                subsidiaries or associated companies of the Company (items (a)
                                and (b), collectively referred to as the “Eligible Participant(s)”).


因根據首次公開發售後股權激勵計劃將授予的所有          The total number of Shares which may be issued upon exercise
購股權及股份獎勵獲行使而可能發行的股份總數合          of all options and share awards to be granted under the Post-

    有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上市。本公司及其             shares were listed on the Main Board of The Stock

    產及銷售白酒及其他酒類產品。                    27 April 2023. The Company and its subsidiaries (together,
                                      the “Group”) are principally engaged in the making,

                                                  及技術專業知識）未予計量。                                  Assistance provided by one segment to
                                                                                                 another, including sharing of assets and

                                                  本集團的其他經營收入及開支                                  The Group’s other operating income and
                                                 （例如其他收益、銷售及經銷                                   expenses, such as other income, selling

                                                  的股權激勵費用、貿易應收款                                  expenses, equity-settled share-based

     下文載列最新年度財務報表所載有關可能對                 The following updates the information provided in the last

     國際財務報告準則第18號，財務報表的列                 IFRS 18, Presentation and disclosure in financial
     報及披露                                statements


     國際財務報告準則第18號將取代國際會計準                IFRS 18 will replace IAS 1, Presentation of financial
     則第 1 號財務報表的呈列 ，旨在提高實體財              statements, and aims to improve the transparency and
     務報表相關信息的透明度及可比性。國際財                 comparability of information about an entity’s financial
     務報告準則第18 號於2027 年1 月1 日或之後          statements. IFRS 18 is effective for annual reporting
     開始的年度報告期間生效並將追溯應用。                  periods beginning on or after 1 January 2027 and is to be
                                         applied retrospectively.


     除其他變動外，根據國際財務報告準則第18                Among other changes, under IFRS 18, entities are required
     號，實體須於損益表中將所有收入及開支分                 to classify all income and expenses into five categories
     類為五個類別，即經營、投資、融資、已終                 in the statement of profit or loss, namely the operating,
     止經營業務及所得稅類別。實體亦須在財務                 investing, financing, discontinued operations and income
     報表的單一附註中提供有關管理層定義的績                 tax categories. Entities are also required to provide specific
     效衡量標準的具體披露。                         disclosures about management-defined performance

                           我們已審閱列載於第68至93頁的中期財務報告，其           We have reviewed the interim financial report set out on pages
                           中包括珍酒李渡集團有限公司（以下簡稱「貴公司」）           68 to 93, which comprises the consolidated statement of financial

                           註。香港聯合交易所有限公司證券上市規則規定，             equity and condensed consolidated cash flow statement for the
                           中期財務報告的編製必須符合上市規則的相關條文             six-month period then ended, and explanatory notes. The Rules
                           以及國際會計準則理事會頒佈的國際會計準則第34            Governing the Listing of Securities on The Stock Exchange of
                           號「中期財務報告 」。董事須負責根據國際會計準則           Hong Kong Limited require the preparation of an interim financial

                                                              International Accounting Standard 34 Interim financial reporting
                                                              as issued by the International Accounting Standards Board. The

                                                              Accounting Standard 34.
